{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"fits","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"fitting","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"fitted","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"fitted","tags":["past"]},{"form":"fit","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"fit","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"It fits the purpose.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To be suitable for."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Ten clowns fit in the car, but not a hundred.","type":"example"},{"text":"The elevator can fit up to 10 people.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To have sufficient space available at some location to be able to be there."],"tags":["ambitransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The small shirt doesn't fit me, so I'll buy the medium size.","type":"example"},{"text":"If I lose a few kilos, the gorgeous wedding dress might fit me.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To conform to in size and shape."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I wanted to borrow my little sister's jeans, but they didn't fit.","type":"example"},{"text":"That plug fitted into the other socket, but it won't go in this one.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To be of the right size and shape"],"synonyms":[{"word":"sit"}],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I want to fit the drapes to the windows.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To make conform in size and shape."],"raw_tags":["with to"],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I had a suit fitted by the tailor.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To make conform in size and shape.","To tailor; to change to the appropriate size."],"raw_tags":["with to"],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"These definitions fit most of the usage.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To be in agreement with."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The regression program fit a line to the data.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To adjust."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To attach, especially when requiring exact positioning or sizing."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The chandler will fit us with provisions for a month.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To equip or supply."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I’m fitting the ship for a summer sail home.","type":"example"},{"text":"I’m fitting to go home and sleep.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To make ready."],"synonyms":[{"word":"fix to"}],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To be seemly."],"tags":["archaic","intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To be proper or becoming."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The paint, the fabrics, the rugs all fit.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To be in harmony."],"synonyms":[{"word":"mesh"}],"tags":["intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/fɪt/"}],"upstream_line":30687,"upstream_offset":207286493,"upstream_record_sha256":"0a9bdc84e68c0f1c5c740ed80e78d4522731f9d519dcb534799ea0b806ad283a","word":"fit"}

{"forms":[{"form":"historical-grammatical","tags":["alternative"]},{"form":"grammatico-historical","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"In biblical exegesis, the historico-grammatical approach tries to avoid extremes: that of dismissing the use of figurative language in the text on the one hand, versus dismissing the possibility of the miraculous and the supernatural on the other; that of ignoring what the original authors intended, versus ignoring relevance for modern readers; that of forcing the text into a preconceived theology, versus interpreting in isolation from the larger story of the gospel.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Pertaining to, using or based on the textual analysis technique of using context and literary form to establish the meaning of the text at the time it was written."],"qualifier":"hermeneutics; textual analysis; hermeneutics; textual analysis","tags":["not-comparable"]}],"upstream_line":7699751,"upstream_offset":17595486205,"upstream_record_sha256":"052af122d268b8ad690256e049f1de3e35a2d9522424139908037f89d8a45ab5","word":"historico-grammatical"}

{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"sags","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"seg","tags":["alternative","dialectal"]},{"form":"segg","tags":["alternative","dialectal"]},{"form":"sack","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["The state of sinking or bending; a droop."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A depression or dip in anything."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A place where the surface of the earth, a mountain range, a ridge, etc., sinks or droops."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The difference in elevation of a wire, cable, chain or rope suspended between two consecutive points."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The difference in height or depth between the vertex and the rim of a curved surface, specifically used for optical elements such as a mirror or lens."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A region or spot where the skin has lost some of its elasticity and droops or hangs down."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A person with sagging skin."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/sæɡ/","tags":["Australia","General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/saɡ/","tags":["British","Northern-England","Scotland","Southern","Standard","Wales"]},{"ipa":"/sæːɡ/","tags":["Australia"]},{"ipa":"/sɛɡ/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/seɪ̯ɡ/"}],"upstream_line":239210,"upstream_offset":1183201485,"upstream_record_sha256":"34d6f71fad0df7112a44a9f9698007f926d02fda356fb395bffa2bc9cecb197d","word":"sag"}
